<div class="aem-Grid aem-Grid--12 aem-Grid--default--12 ">
    
    <div class="richtext text cmp-bottom-spacing-40 aem-GridColumn aem-GridColumn--default--12">
<div data-icon-aria-label=" (opens in a new tab)" id="richtext-7e86932e96" class="cmp-text rich-text-container">
    <div class="rich-text-wrapper">
        <h2 class="richtext--element-font"><span class="variant headlineLarge"><b>Frequently asked questions</b></span></h2>
 
        <!-- Used unsafe context as temporary fix and create adobe support ticket to
        figure out why html context adding extra ul and li elements -->
    </div>
</div>

    





    

</div>
<div class="accordion aem-GridColumn aem-GridColumn--default--12">
<div>
    <dig-accordion data-cmp-multiple-expansion>
        
            <dig-accordion-item slot="item" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_accordionItems_item0">
                What makes AI tax software effective for professionals?
                <dig-accordion-panel slot="panel" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_accordionItems_item0">
                    <div slot="content">
                        <dig-typography semantic variant="bodyMedium" color="secondary" element="p">
                            <p>The most effective AI tax software for professionals is typically offered as software as a service (SaaS), like CoCounsel Tax. This model ensures that users receive regular updates, expanded capabilities, and ongoing support, making it an ideal choice for staying current with evolving tax regulations and best practices. By providing continuous improvements and enhancements, this type of service helps tax professionals maintain accuracy, efficiency, and compliance in their work.</p>
                        </dig-typography>
                    </div>
                </dig-accordion-panel>
            </dig-accordion-item>
        
            <dig-accordion-item slot="item" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_accordionItems_item1">
                How can I use artificial intelligence for tax research?
                <dig-accordion-panel slot="panel" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_accordionItems_item1">
                    <div slot="content">
                        <dig-typography semantic variant="bodyMedium" color="secondary" element="p">
                            <p><p>Leverage artificial intelligence with CoCounsel Tax, an innovative AI tax assistant that enables you to conduct comprehensive tax research, analyze complex tax scenarios, and identify potential tax-savings opportunities. </p>
<p>With CoCounsel Tax, you can:</p>
<ul><li>Conduct thorough tax research using authoritative sources like the IRS code and Checkpoint Catalyst.</li><li>Analyze complex tax scenarios, including entity classification and pass-through entity taxation.</li><li>Identify potential tax-savings opportunities, such as cost segregation studies.</li></ul>
<p>By utilizing CoCounsel Tax, you can streamline your tax research process, reduce manual work, and improve the accuracy of your research — allowing you to focus on delivering high-quality advice to your clients and growing your practice.</p>
</p>
                        </dig-typography>
                    </div>
                </dig-accordion-panel>
            </dig-accordion-item>
        
            <dig-accordion-item slot="item" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_accordionItems_item2">
                Is CoCounsel Tax secure?
                <dig-accordion-panel slot="panel" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_accordionItems_item2">
                    <div slot="content">
                        <dig-typography semantic variant="bodyMedium" color="secondary" element="p">
                            <p>Yes. We’ve designed CoCounsel Tax with security in mind, ensuring that your client data and research are protected.</p>
                        </dig-typography>
                    </div>
                </dig-accordion-panel>
            </dig-accordion-item>
        
            <dig-accordion-item slot="item" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_accordionItems_item3">
                Can CoCounsel Tax work with my existing workflows?
                <dig-accordion-panel slot="panel" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_accordionItems_item3">
                    <div slot="content">
                        <dig-typography semantic variant="bodyMedium" color="secondary" element="p">
                            <p>Yes. We’ve designed CoCounsel Tax to operate seamlessly with your existing tools and processes, streamlining your tax research and planning workflows.</p>
                        </dig-typography>
                    </div>
                </dig-accordion-panel>
            </dig-accordion-item>
        
            <dig-accordion-item slot="item" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_accordionItems_item4">
                How can CoCounsel Tax help me improve my tax practice?
                <dig-accordion-panel slot="panel" value="_root_container_copy_61054296_accordion_accordionItems_item4">
                    <div slot="content">
                        <dig-typography semantic variant="bodyMedium" color="secondary" element="p">
                            <p>CoCounsel Tax provides accurate and reliable research, identifies potential tax-savings opportunities, and streamlines your workflows. By optimizing your operations, it allows you to focus on delivering high-quality advice to your clients.</p>
                        </dig-typography>
                    </div>
                </dig-accordion-panel>
            </dig-accordion-item>
        
    </dig-accordion>

                <script type="text/javascript">
// Hide OneTrust banner if wcmmode=disabled is present in URL or in PROD preview domain
if (window.location.search.includes('wcmmode=disabled') || window.location.host.includes('preview-prod-cs.ewp.thomsonreuters.com')) {
    const style = document.createElement('style');
    style.textContent = '[id*="onetrust"],[class*="onetrust"],[id*="ot-"],[class*="ot-"]{display:none!important}';
    document.head.appendChild(style);
}

// function to pull cookie value
function getCookie(name) {
    var value = "; " + document.cookie;
    var parts = value.split("; " + name + "=");
    if (parts.length == 2) return parts.pop().split(";").shift();
}
function OptanonWrapper() { 
        setTimeout(() => {
            _satellite.pageBottom();
        }, 1000);
    var OABCcookieName = "OptanonConsent";
    var bannerAcceptBtn = document.getElementById("onetrust-accept-btn-handler");
    var pcAllowAllBtn = document.getElementById("accept-recommended-btn-handler");
    var pcSaveBtn = document.getElementsByClassName("save-preference-btn-handler onetrust-close-btn-handler")[0];
    var OABCcookie = getCookie(OABCcookieName);
        
   
    // IF logic needed here because ot-banner-sdk DIV is not injected on page loads if banner is not exposed
    if (!OABCcookie && bannerAcceptBtn) {
        bannerAcceptBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
            location.reload();
        });
    }
    if (pcAllowAllBtn)
        pcAllowAllBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
            location.reload();
        });
    if(pcSaveBtn){
        pcSaveBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
            setTimeout(()=> {
            location.reload();
        }, 1000) //quick timeout so that the consent receipt can be sent and the cookie can be updated
        });
    }
} </script>
